+Low skilled tasks
+Excludes any position relating to mass or standardised production (as opposed to specialist production), including the following:
+a position based in a franchise or factory
+a position that:
+involves full or partial production of food product for distribution to another location or
+predominantly involves the use of pre‑prepared food product from another location

+Low skilled tasks
+Excludes any position involved in mass production in a factory setting
+Low skilled tasks
+Excludes any position in a limited service restaurant.
+A limited service restaurant includes, but is not limited to:
+a fast food or takeaway food service
+a fast casual restaurant
+a drinking establishment that offers only a limited food service
+a limited service cafe, including a coffee shop or mall cafe
+a limited service pizza restaurant.

+Low skilled tasks
+Excludes any position in a limited service restaurant.
+A limited service restaurant includes, but is not limited to:
+a fast food or takeaway food service
+a fast casual restaurant

+a limited service pizza restaurant.

+482 - Skills in Demand (subclass 482) - Core Skills stream
+Low skilled tasks
+Excludes any position that:
+is based in a front‑line retail setting or
+predominantly involves direct client transactional interaction on a regular basis or
+predominantly involves selling educational courses to individual students or
+is based in a call centre and does not require a significant technical knowledge of products.
+Business size
+Excludes any position where:
+the position is in a business that has an annual turnover of less than AUD1,000,000 and
+if the nominee is to be transferred to fill the position—the transfer is not an intra‑corporate transfer to which an international trade obligation applies.
+the position is in a business that has fewer than 5 employees and
+if the nominee is to be transferred to fill the position—the transfer is not an intra‑corporate transfer to which an international trade obligation applies.
